850 entries, llm-wiki-finite to lmm-scripts. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **llm-wiki-finite**: Repository LLM-wiki workflows for ingesting source collections, maintaining topic-scoped wiki directories, querying compiled knowledge, auditing provenance, and generating outputs. Ordinary Personal Brain/wiki knowledge-base requests use finitebrain. Use when the user wants to challenge a plan against documented LLP decisions before writing code. ([hyparam/hypaware](https://github.com/hyparam/hypaware/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/llp-grill) · ★17)
- **llp-init**: Add LLP to a new repository by scaffolding the initial llp/ tree, drafting LLP 0000, and setting up agent instructions that keep the system maintained as code is written. ([hyparam/hypaware](https://github.com/hyparam/hypaware/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/llp-init) · ★17)
- **llp-init-retrofit**: Add LLP to an existing repository by surveying the codebase and docs, drafting the root LLP, and proposing a phased adoption and annotation plan. ([hyparam/hypaware](https://github.com/hyparam/hypaware/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/llp-init-retrofit) · ★17)
- **llp-list**: List the LLP documents in this repository, grouped by status or filtered by type, system, or status. Useful for answering "what's still in draft?", "which RFCs are active?", or "what documents cover the auth system?" ([hyparam/hypaware](https://github.com/hyparam/hypaware/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/llp-list) · ★17)
- **llp-maintain**: Keep an LLP corpus in sync with its code. Detects code/doc drift, validates @ref annotations interactively, checks provenance tags, and proposes reconciliations, ref repairs, status changes, and overlay changes. Modes — pre-pr, audit, reconcile, retire-proposal, curate (the promote → archive → realign pass). Proposes; never applies. ([expo/exact](https://github.com/expo/exact/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/llp-maintain) · ★4.7k · 2 copies)
- **llp-maintain**: Use when LLP docs and code may have drifted: before a PR in an LLP repo, when a doc disagrees with code, or to audit @refs, rename or retire an LLP, or curate llp/current. Proposes fixes, never applies them. ([peterpme/skills](https://github.com/peterpme/skills/tree/HEAD/llp-maintain) · ★29)
- **llp-orient**: Before editing a subsystem, file, or starting a task, assemble the governing LLP context — read the root LLP, follow the @ref annotations on the code in scope, and find the LLPs covering the relevant systems. Produces a compact context pack of the constraints a change must respect. Read-only. ([expo/exact](https://github.com/expo/exact/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/llp-orient) · ★4.7k · 2 copies)
- **llp-orient**: Use before editing code or starting a task in a repo with an llp/ folder: gathers the root LLP and the LLPs the code's @refs point to, and returns the constraints the change must respect. ([peterpme/skills](https://github.com/peterpme/skills/tree/HEAD/llp-orient) · ★29)
- **llp-review**: Run the LLP 0005 review loop for an LLP document — orchestrate reviews from other model families, write one provenance-tracked artifact per review actually received under llp/reviews/, and never fabricate a review or accept on the author's behalf. Review intensity scales with stakes; the author decides when it's enough. ([expo/exact](https://github.com/expo/exact/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/llp-review) · ★4.7k · 3 copies)
- **llp-review**: Review an existing LLP document using a standard review prompt that asks about strengths, concerns, missing considerations, and open questions. Saves the review as a dated artifact for later reference. ([hyparam/hypaware](https://github.com/hyparam/hypaware/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/llp-review) · ★17)
- **llp-ship**: Use when Peter types /llp-ship or hands over decided work to build and land in an LLP repo: Linear tickets, ticket files, tasks, or an accepted LLP ("it's accepted, implement it"). Builds in worktrees, integrates, verifies, delivers. ([peterpme/skills](https://github.com/peterpme/skills/tree/HEAD/llp-ship) · ★29)
- **llp-super-refine**: Author-invoked dual-family refine loop for one or more Draft/Review LLPs — each round, two fresh mutually-blind reviews from different model families, orchestrator revision, verdict-lined artifacts — until both families mark the same revision READY, a delta round finds no MATERIAL concern in pre-existing text across families, or a bounded stop rule escalates to the author. Use when the user invokes /llp-super-refine or asks to refine an LLP until independent models agree it is ready. ([expo/exact](https://github.com/expo/exact/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/llp-super-refine) · ★4.7k)
- **llp-super-refine**: Use when Peter types /llp-super-refine or wants a Draft or Review LLP refined until two model families independently mark the same revision READY: rounds of blind reviews and revisions with a bounded stop rule. For a single review round, use llp-review. ([peterpme/skills](https://github.com/peterpme/skills/tree/HEAD/llp-super-refine) · ★29)
- **llr**: Generates Low-Level Requirements (LLR) from a PRD and feature matrix. Each LLR maps to a specific feature with testable acceptance criteria. Output written to artifacts/{req-id}/tier2-ba/llr-document.md Example: /llr REQ-001 ([ravir2cognizant/client_demo_rag](https://github.com/ravir2cognizant/client_demo_rag/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/llr))
- **lls-ai-data-analysis-starter**: 带普通用户把 Excel、CSV 或表格问题转成可复核的数据分析。用于第一次做数据清洗、描述统计、分组比较、趋势、异常检查、图表和结论汇报时；必须先明确业务问题、字段含义、时间范围与统计口径，再检查数据质量并生成可重复步骤，不把相关性写成因果，也不根据缺失数据编造结论。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-ai-data-analysis-starter) · ★5)
- **lls-ai-drawing-prompt-coach**: 把用户的模糊画面想法整理成可测试、可迭代的文生图提示词和视觉方案。用于角色、场景、商品氛围图、插画、封面素材或概念图创作时；必须先明确画面用途、主体、叙事瞬间、构图、光线、色彩与限制，一次只调整一个变量并记录结果，不堆砌互相冲突的风格词。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-ai-drawing-prompt-coach) · ★5)
- **lls-ai-microdrama-planner**: 把一个题材想法发展成可连续生产的 AI 漫剧或短剧方案。用于确定受众承诺、人物欲望、冲突引擎、单集节拍、悬念钩子、分镜资产与连续性规则时；必须区分故事设定、已发生事实和待验证假设，不用随机反转、旁白堆积或视觉奇观代替因果推进。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-ai-microdrama-planner) · ★5)
- **lls-ai-novel-prompt-engineer**: 为中长篇小说建立可持续迭代的提示词与状态管理系统。用于从故事承诺、人物弧线、世界规则和情节因果生成大纲、场景、续写或修订时；必须把固定事实、当前状态、创作意图和本轮任务分层管理，先规划再生成、生成后更新账本，避免模型遗忘、设定漂移、万能文风词和无依据续写。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-ai-novel-prompt-engineer) · ★5)
- **lls-ai-poster-design-coach**: 把活动、课程、产品或品牌信息整理成可执行的海报内容与版式方案。用于需要明确传播目标、信息优先级、文案、尺寸、安全区、视觉方向、生成素材和导出验收时；必须先建立信息层级，再设计版式和图像，不把全部文字塞进画面，也不依赖图像模型生成准确正文。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-ai-poster-design-coach) · ★5)

- **lls-aida-marketing-copywriter**: 用 AIDA 结构把真实产品、课程、活动或服务材料写成可验证的营销文案。用于广告、落地页、私域消息、海报文案和短视频转化段落时；必须先明确受众处境、产品事实、可信证据、渠道和行动成本，再组织注意、兴趣、渴望与行动，不制造虚假稀缺、伪造评价、夸张收益或隐藏重要限制。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-aida-marketing-copywriter) · ★5)

- **lls-chinese-humor**: 把已经明确的中文课程、口播、文章或现场表达改得更轻松、更有记忆点。用于根据受众、关系、场合和原意选择反差、错位、回扣、自嘲、具体类比等幽默机制，并控制密度、落点和可朗读性时；必须先确定谁可以成为笑点对象，不拿创伤、疾病、弱势身份、客户失误和私人信息换笑声，也不用网络烂梗破坏可信度。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-chinese-humor) · ★5)

- **lls-dialogue-subtext-reader**: 分析聊天、会议、反馈或协商中的言外之意、关系信号和未明说诉求。用于用户问“他这句话什么意思”“这段对话哪里不对”“我该怎么回”时；必须区分原话事实、合理推测和未知信息，给出多种解释与低冲突回应，不把推测写成心理诊断或确定事实。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-dialogue-subtext-reader) · ★5)

- **lls-headline-summary-maker**: 基于已经核验的正文、视频或报告，为特定渠道生成清楚、有吸引力但不夸大的标题、副标题和多长度摘要。用于提炼核心承诺、建立事实边界、生成不同标题机制、评估歧义与点击后兑现度、适配搜索/社交/报告场景时；必须从源材料提取可证明主张，不凭空添加数字、身份、结果或紧迫性。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-headline-summary-maker) · ★5)

- **lls-international-techdoc-writer**: 把原始材料重构为面向任务、警示可执行、术语一致并适合翻译与交付的技术文档。 当用户面对相应复杂任务并需要可验证结果时使用。 ([PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills](https://github.com/PhilRobinluo/ai-coevolution-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/lls-international-techdoc-writer) · ★5)
